Well... I mean, I really hope I don't come across as someone who just throws a pity party for himself, because I know I should be stronger than this. But, um... yes, I do feel that way. A lot, actually.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, I... I really don't want to sound like a stick in the mud, you know? I still try to be good company for my wife. But, um... I guess if I'm being completely honest, I haven't been doing much of what I used to love. 

I used to really look forward to going bowling, or getting outside and working in the garden. And I always loved taking my wife out to gallery shows or going dancing with her. Lately, though... I just kind of sit there. I end up making up excuses not to go, because... well, none of it really feels worth the effort right now. 

I feel terrible admitting that out loud, because I know I should be taking her out and doing those things. I hope that doesn't make me sound like a bad husband. It's probably just a phase, right? Everyone gets a little burnt out on their hobbies sometimes, don't you think? I really am trying my best to get back into the swing of things, I promise.

Well, I... I try to look for the silver lining, you know? I really do. I don't want to be the kind of person who just complains all the time. But... um... if I'm being honest, I can't really think of anything lately that's... well, that's made me feel good.

Well, I... I try to get a good night's rest, I really do. I know how important it is to be sharp for work, and I don't want to be dragging around the house, making my wife worry. I always make sure to get to bed at a reasonable hour.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, I... no, I don't think so. I've always prided myself on being a pretty upbeat guy. You know, working in sales, you kind of have to keep a smile on your face and keep pushing forward for the clients. I never want to be the guy bringing everyone else down.
